All daily except the four monthly cost-of-debt series. **`TreasurySeriesEntry` dataclass + parser block** in `src/genkei/common/watchlist.py` with `find_treasury(series_id)` lookup helper on `Watchlist`; per-entry dedup on `series_id` upper-case and explicit frequency-set validation against `{D, W, M, Q, A}` at parse time. **Health integration** — `treasury` registered in `PRIMARY_TABLES` (pointing at `treasury.observations`) and `RECURRING_ENDPOINTS` (with both `collect` and `normalize`). `ExpectationsRegistryTests` updated to pin both registries. **Daily cron** (`treasury-daily.yml`) at **12:00 UTC** on `[self-hosted, beelink]` — 30 min after BEA-daily so the free-API macro trio (FRED → BEA → Treasury) stays staggered. Daily polling matches the daily endpoints (`debt_to_penny`, `operating_cash_balance`); monthly endpoints upsert cleanly on no-change days. No GH Actions secret needed for the API itself — only `GENKEI_DATABASE_URL`. **Tests 1645 → 1726 (+81, all unit), ruff clean on the new files**: 24 collector tests across 5 classes (module constants, EndpointTarget blob-endpoint slug shape covering v2/v1/uppercase paths, load_targets dedup + sort + empty-section guard, build_page_url canonical params + pagination, _extract_total_pages covering int / string / missing / negative / unparseable, _fetch_all_pages covering page-merging / short-page termination / total-pages-1 termination / non-dict / missing-data / MAX_PAGES guard); 42 normalizer tests across 7 classes (parse_value covering numeric strings / thousands-separators / null sentinels / N/A / empty / garbage / native numerics / None / booleans; parse_record_date covering ISO strings / year boundaries / native date / naive + TZ-aware datetimes / invalid strings / non-string types; row_matches_filter covering empty filter / single + multi field match / mismatch / missing field / null field / numeric coercion; normalize_endpoint covering single-row + multi-series projection / metadata persistence / row_filter dropping / missing-value null landing / missing-value-field raise / unparseable date drop + raise / missing-series raise / within-blob dedup last-wins / malformed payload raise; _endpoint_to_blob_name round-trip; _series_by_endpoint grouping; _validate_blob_coverage missing + present; _validate_source_run_row covering missing / wrong source / wrong endpoint / failed status / partial endpoint / clean success). All 66 tests pass against synthetic payloads; **live verification deferred** until the cron fires (no API key gate this time — Treasury is fully open). **Docs** — `docs/sources/treasury.md` (~220 lines) records the v1 9-series watchlist by dimension, the deferred `auctions_query` v2 path, the endpoint contract (base URL, no-auth, pagination shape, response envelope, date/numeric formats), the schema with friendly-`series_id` rationale + JSONB `row_filter` design + no-frequency-in-PK call, seven v1 limitations a consumer should know (latest-only, no backfill flag, `"null"` sentinel, thousands-sep values, TGA pre-2022 coverage limitation, polite-default rate limit, no 200-OK-with-error handling), how it runs (cron + manual replay + health integration), the current query path (`genkei query` over `treasury.observations`), and the natural follow-ups (`auctions_query` v2 event table, pre-2022 TGA legacy series, cost-of-debt cross-check experiment, typed CLI surface, B-066 regime classifier extension with TGA + avg-rate inputs). **Deferred follow-ups** (filed implicitly via the v1 scope calls): `auctions_query` v2 needs a separate `treasury.auctions` event table; pre-2022 TGA backfill via a `TGA_CLOSING_BAL_LEGACY` series with a different `row_filter` against the pre-format-change `account_type` strings; typed `genkei fiscal --series <id>` or standalone `genkei treasury` CLI subcommand (today's path is `genkei query` + raw SQL); B-066 regime classifier extension to consume TGA Δ + avg-rate trajectory once enough history accumulates. **Phase 2 progress**: with B-030 closed, the remaining Phase 2 items in the priority tier are EIA energy (B-032) and per-source docs (B-036); everything else is paid-data gated (B-084) or TOS-blocked (B-104).
- **Evidence:** `migrations/versions/20260611_create_treasury_schema.py` (`treasury` schema, `treasury.series` with `row_filter` JSONB column + frequency CHECK, `treasury.observations` hypertable on `ts` with 90-day chunks + 30-day compression policy + `segmentby=series_id`). `src/genkei/common/watchlist.py` (`TreasurySeriesEntry` dataclass with default `date_field='record_date'` and ### ~~B-032 — EIA energy data ingester~~
- **Resolved:** 2026-06-11 (branch eia-energy, commits `168982c` + `9c8c573` + `875d2b9` + `75503d4` + `3fa42da` + `68f72c5` + `9332fc3` + `2517986`)
- **Outcome:** Lands the **energy dimension** of the macro stack — oil, natural gas, and electricity — completing the free-API macro quartet alongside FRED (rates/credit/vol/FX, B-028), BEA (real-economy growth, B-029), and Treasury (debt/cash/cost-of-debt, B-030). v1 ships **11 curated series across 3 sleeves** of the open EIA Open Data v2 API. Three load-bearing sleeves are wired: **petroleum** (daily WTI + Brent spot via `petroleum/pri/spt`; weekly commercial crude ex-SPR + gasoline + distillate + SPR inventories via `petroleum/stoc/wstk`; monthly US crude production via `petroleum/crd/crpdn`), **natural gas** (daily Henry Hub spot via `natural-gas/pri/fut`; weekly Lower-48 working storage via `natural-gas/stor/wkly`; monthly US marketed production via `natural-gas/prod/sum`), and **electricity** (monthly US net generation, all sectors, all fuels via `electricity/electric-power-operational-data`). **Scope decisions** (all four confirmed up-front via AskUserQuestion): (1) **Core oil/gas/power, ~8-12 series** (vs narrower 4-5 or broader 25-40) — keeps the macro-relevant signal tight without baking in PADD-region or per-fuel cardinality that no current experiment needs. (2) **EIA_API_KEY required, registration is free + instant** — same env-var + GH-Actions-secret pattern as FRED + BEA. (3) **10-year backfill** — matches Treasury + BEA; covers the 2014 oil-price crash + 2020 COVID demand collapse + the post-2022 shale-vs-OPEC pivot. (4) **Latest-only, NOT vintage-aware** — matches BEA + Treasury; EIA's v2 API has no vintage/as-of parameter (revisions overwrite in place at the source, especially monthly STEO + production data). PK is `(series_id, ts)` with no vintage column. **Schema** (migration `a2b3c4d55667`): new `eia` schema with `eia.series` (entity dim, PK `series_id` TEXT) and `eia.observations` (time-series fact, hypertable on `ts` with 90-day chunks matching FRED + BEA + Treasury, compression > 30 days with `segmentby=series_id`). `series_id` is a **friendly TEXT key curated in the watchlist** (e.g. `WTI_SPOT`, `CRUDE_INV_EXSPR`, `HH_SPOT`, `ELEC_NET_GEN_US`) — EIA v2 organizes data by route + facets rather than a single legacy series identifier, so a friendly local key reads better than the inherited `RWTC`/`WCESTUS1`/`NW2_EPG0_SWO_R48_BCF` style. **`facets` is JSONB** on `eia.series` so consumers can see at a glance which facet projection a series selects (e.g. `ELEC_NET_GEN_US` pins `{fueltype: ALL, location: US, sectorid: '99'}` to extract US-wide all-fuel net generation from the multi-faceted operational-data route). **`frequency` is NOT in the PK** (matches Treasury, departs from BEA) — each EIA series is locked to a single cadence by construction. CHECK constraint pins `frequency IN ('D', 'W', 'M', 'Q', 'A')`. **`data_field` is per-entry** — most routes return `value` but electricity operational data uses `generation`; the watchlist binds whichever column EIA exposes for that route. **Collector** (`src/genkei/ingest/eia.py`): hits `https://api.eia.gov/v2/<route>/data/?api_key=KEY&frequency=daily|weekly|...&data[0]=<field>&start=YYYY-MM-DD&sort[0][column]=period&sort[0][direction]=asc&offset=N&length=5000&facets[KEY][]=VALUE&...` — **one URL per series**, not per route. Multiple series may share a route (`petroleum/stoc/wstk` covers WCESTUS1 / WGTSTUS1 / WDISTUS1 / WCSSTUS1) but their facets differ, and one-call-per-series keeps each blob scoped to a single watched series so the normalizer can dispatch by blob slug without route-level row sieving. **Pagination**: `_fetch_all_pages` loops via `offset` + `length=5000` using the response's `response.total` as the source of truth (parsed as int OR string defensively — EIA returns both shapes), with a short-page fallback when total is absent and a `MAX_PAGES=200` runaway guard. The combined payload merges every page's `response.data` array into a single object so the normalizer sees one canonical blob per series. **API-key redaction mirrors FRED's G-015 fix** — `api_key=KEY` lives in the query string, so URLs landed in `meta.raw_blobs.url` go through `_redact_key` to replace the key with `***` before insert. The audit table stays safe to share for replay/debug; the redactor also strips any incidental key leakage from error-message strings before they land in the partial-endpoints log. **Rate limit**: 2 req/s — EIA's anonymous tier allows 5,000 req/hour, but the polite default matches our FRED + BEA + Treasury convention and 11 series × ~1 page each completes in well under a minute. **Blob-endpoint slug** (`eia_<series_id_lower>`, e.g. `eia_wti_spot`) reverse-maps to the watchlist entry deterministically. **Normalizer** (`src/genkei/normalize/eia.py`): per-blob dispatch by reverse-slug lookup; loads the watchlist + indexes each entry by its blob slug for O(1) per-blob lookup. For each row in the series payload, applies the watchlist `facets` filter defensively (in case a route returns mixed projections), parses the period via the per-frequency parser, and emits an observation. **`parse_period` handles five EIA period shapes**: `D`/`W` → `YYYY-MM-DD` (UTC midnight; weekly preserves the week-ending day as published), `M` → `YYYY-MM` (month-start), `Q` → `YYYY-Qn` (quarter-start; rejects quarters > 4), `A` → `YYYY` (year-start). Already-typed `date`/`datetime` objects pass through with UTC normalization for defensive cases. **`parse_value` handles EIA's mixed native + string numerics**: native `int`/`float` pass through, strings get thousands-separator stripping + scientific-notation support, `null`/`N/A`/`na`/`-`/empty strings collapse to `None`, booleans rejected defensively. **Facet filter** (`_row_matches_facets`) is case-sensitive via `str(...)` coercion so the watchlist YAML doesn't have to care about numeric vs string facet values (EIA returns `sectorid` as int in some routes, string in others). **No-matching-rows raise** — if a watched series's blob produces zero observations after facet filtering, the normalizer raises rather than silently emitting an empty result; catching contract drift loud is the point. **Within-period dedup** — last value per period wins; defensive for EIA's occasional intra-payload revision rows. **Watchlist seed** — 11 series across 3 sleeves under three dimension comment blocks: petroleum spot prices (`WTI_SPOT`, `BRENT_SPOT`), petroleum inventories + production (`CRUDE_INV_EXSPR`, `GASOLINE_INV`, `DISTILLATE_INV`, `SPR_CRUDE`, `CRUDE_PRODUCTION_US`), natural gas (`HH_SPOT`, `NG_STORAGE_L48`, `NG_MARKETED_PROD_US`), and electricity (`ELEC_NET_GEN_US`). Cadences mix daily (4), weekly (5), and monthly (3). **`EiaSeriesEntry` dataclass + parser block** in `src/genkei/common/watchlist.py` with `find_eia(series_id)` lookup helper on `Watchlist`; per-entry dedup on `series_id` upper-case and explicit frequency-set validation against `{D, W, M, Q, A}` at parse time. Optional `data_field` (defaults to `value`) + optional `facets` `Mapping[str, str]` cover the full EIA v2 surface needed today. **Health integration** — `eia` registered in `PRIMARY_TABLES` (pointing at `eia.observations`) and `RECURRING_ENDPOINTS` (with both `collect` and `normalize`). `ExpectationsRegistryTests` updated to pin both registries. **Daily cron** (`eia-daily.yml`) at **12:30 UTC** on `[self-hosted, beelink]` — 30 min after Treasury-daily so the free-API macro quartet (FRED 11:00 → BEA 11:30 → Treasury 12:00 → EIA 12:30) stays staggered. Daily polling matches the daily spot prices (`WTI_SPOT` / `BRENT_SPOT` / `HH_SPOT`); weekly inventories publish Wednesdays + Thursdays; monthly endpoints upsert cleanly on no-change days. Reads `EIA_API_KEY` from GH Actions secrets + `GENKEI_DATABASE_URL`. **Tests 1726 → 1812 (+86, all unit), ruff clean on the new files**: 31 collector tests across 8 classes (module constants, `_eia_frequency` short→verbose mapping, `SeriesTarget` blob-endpoint slug shape, `load_targets` watchlist projection + sort + electricity-facet preservation + empty-section guard, `build_page_url` canonical params + offset increments + multi-facet emission + URL stability across calls, `_redact_key` covering basic + empty-key + repeated-occurrence paths, `_extract_total` covering int / string / missing / negative / unparseable, `_fetch_all_pages` covering total-driven merging / short-page termination / offset increments / non-dict / missing-response-block / missing-data-array); 46 normalizer tests across 6 classes (`parse_value` covering native float / numeric strings / scientific notation / thousands-separators / null sentinels / N/A / empty / garbage / native int / None / booleans, `parse_period` covering all five frequencies + invalid quarter + empty + non-string + naive-datetime UTC attachment + tz-aware passthrough, `_row_matches_facets` covering empty filter / single + multi facet match / mismatch / missing key / numeric coercion, `normalize_series` covering canonical projection / facet filtering / last-value-per-period / per-series-data-field for electricity / no-rows raise / missing-data-field raise / null-value passthrough / non-dict + missing-response + missing-data defensive raises, `_series_blob_name` matching collector's blob_endpoint, `_series_by_blob_name` indexing all entries, `_validate_blob_coverage` present + missing, `_validate_source_run_row` covering missing / wrong source / wrong endpoint / failed status / partial endpoint / clean success). All 77 new tests pass against synthetic payloads; **live verification deferred** until `EIA_API_KEY` is registered + added to GH Actions secrets and the cron fires. **Docs** — `docs/sources/eia.md` (~165 lines) records the v1 11-series watchlist by sleeve + cadence, four deferred route categories (STEO short-term outlook, state/PADD-level series, hourly RTO electricity, per-fuel breakdowns), the endpoint contract (base URL, route + facets model, auth shape, pagination, frequency mapping), the schema with friendly-`series_id` + JSONB `facets` rationale, four v1 limitations a consumer should know (10y default backfill, ALL-fuel electricity scope, API-key redaction, rate-limit politeness), how it runs (cron + manual replay + health integration + collect→normalize chaining), the current query path (`genkei query` over `eia.observations`), and the natural follow-ups (per-fuel electricity facets, STEO forecast schema, state/PADD breakdown, typed `genkei eia` CLI subcommand). **Deferred follow-ups** (filed implicitly via the v1 scope calls): typed `genkei eia --series <id>` or extension to `genkei macro --source eia` CLI subcommand (today's path is `genkei query` + raw SQL); per-fuel electricity entries (`ELEC_NET_GEN_COAL` / `NUC` / `SUN` / `WND`) — watchlist-only addition, no code change; STEO forecast schema if forward EIA forecasts ever become useful (separate `eia.forecast_observations` hypertable with `(series_id, forecast_date, observation_date)` PK); state / PADD-level breakdown when regional refinery analysis needs it; B-066 regime classifier extension to consume oil / gas / electricity inputs once enough history accumulates. **Phase 2 progress**: with B-032 closed, the free-API macro quartet (FRED + BEA + Treasury + EIA) is fully wired. The only remaining Phase 2 ingester item in the priority tier is per-source docs (B-036); everything else is paid-data gated (B-084) or TOS-blocked (B-104).
- **Evidence:** `migrations/versions/20260611_create_eia_schema.py` (`eia` schema, `eia.series` with `facets` JSONB column + frequency CHECK, `eia.observations` hypertable on `ts` with 90-day chunks + 30-day compression policy + `segmentby=series_id`). `src/genkei/common/watchlist.py` (`EiaSeriesEntry` dataclass with default `data_field='value'` + `date_field='period'` + `Mapping[str, str]` `facets`, `find_eia` lookup helper, `eia:` parser block with `series_id` upper-case dedup + frequency-set validation + facet dict projection). `src/genkei/data/watchlists.yml` (new `eia:` section, 11 curated series across 3 sleeves under three dimension comment blocks). `src/genkei/ingest/eia.py` (collector: `SeriesTarget` dataclass with deterministic blob-endpoint slug from lowercased series_id, `load_targets` watchlist-driven projection with stable sort, `require_api_key` env reader with signup hint, `build_page_url` with stable facet-key ordering + URL-encoded EIA params, `_eia_frequency` ### ~~B-036 — Per-source ingest documentation~~
- **Resolved:** 2026-06-12 (branch ingest-documentation, commits `69e5b12` + `0e04015` + `c4297c9` + `b55737b`)
- **Outcome:** Backfills `docs/sources/<name>.md` documentation for **every shipped ingester** that didn't already carry a doc — 12 new files across the four free-API macro pulls (FRED, DefiLlama), crypto market data (CoinGecko, Coinbase), positioning + news (CFTC COT, GDELT GKG), equity OHLCV (Yahoo), the SEC EDGAR family (companyfacts / Form 4 / Form 13F), and the onchain staking pair (Chainlink v0.2, Sui validators). Combined with the six docs already in `docs/sources/` (bea, eia, eth-whale-addresses, spot-etf-net-flow, sui-unlocks, treasury), the lake's free-source coverage is now **fully documented at 18 source docs**. Each file follows the template established by `docs/sources/treasury.md` + `docs/sources/eia.md` + `docs/sources/bea.md`: **coverage v1 table** binding watchlist sections to endpoints + cadences + units; **endpoint contract** (base URL, auth, rate limit, response shape); **schema** rationale (PKs, hypertable choice, segmentby, units); **v1 limitations & known issues** (contract-drift guardrails, missing-value sentinels, API-key redaction notes, single-mode vs backfill semantics); **how it runs** (cron + GH Actions workflow path + reads); **query path** (typed CLI surface + `genkei query` fallback); **acceptance gates** (5-6 numbered checks mirroring `docs/defillama-daily-review.md` — freshness, watchlist coverage, latest-row staleness band, no-partial-endpoint guard, PK invariants); **follow-ups** (deferred capabilities, known v2 work, related backlog items). **Acceptance-gate pattern** propagates the DeFiLlama daily-review discipline across every source: each doc now carries a fixed checklist a research session can run before consuming the source's signals — protects against the silent-drift failure mode that 4 sources hit for ~4 days in G-027/G-028/D-020 before `genkei watchlist health` shipped. **Per-source highlights captured in docs**: DefiLlama's day-aligned timestamp fix (20260604 migration) + Bitcoin exclusion-keyword filter; FRED's vintage-aware PK (D-013) + 2000-vintage JSON cap workaround; CoinGecko's three-tier auth + public keyless live-fetch mode; Coinbase's column-order quirk (low/high before open/close) + 300-candle cap; CFTC's Tuesday-snapshot / Friday-publication lag-shift requirement to avoid lookahead bias; GDELT's 365-day server-side retention mirror + watchlist-driven match filter; Yahoo's browser-UA requirement + `adj_close` vs `close` semantics; SEC's `SEC_USER_AGENT` fair-access rule (G-022) + 200-OK-with-error envelope; Form 4's seven-letter transaction-code set + xsl* prefix-stripping; Form 13F's two-phase (submissions + per-filing) fetch + ×1000 USD conversion + holdings-bearing vs notice-only differentiation; onchain-staking's cap-and-intent dynamics insight (Community pool net flow structurally zero in steady state, use `UnbondingPeriodStarted` count for demand) + LINK-unit `amount_token` representation; sui-staking's forward-only constraint (public fullnode doesn't expose historical epochs, no backfill possible without indexer API) + MIST units (1 SUI = 10⁹ MIST) + `NUMERIC(40, 0)` column type to handle max-supply overflow. **Tests 1812 → 1812 (no test changes — pure documentation), ruff clean** (docs not subject to ruff, but src + tests re-validated). **No new schemas, code, or workflows** — this is the documentation-only counterpart to the architectural completeness check; every shipped ingester now has a future-maintainer-readable doc covering what it does, how it runs, what it can't do, and what the next consumer should verify before trusting its output. **Deferred follow-ups** (consistent across the docs as appropriate): typed per-source CLI subcommands (today most paths route through `genkei query` + raw SQL); historical-backfill where the source allows it (most macro/equity sources already covered; Sui staking structurally blocked); B-066 regime classifier extension consuming the new dimensions (EIA energy, BEA growth, Treasury cost-of-debt) once enough history accumulates; watchlist CUSIP coverage so Form 13F holdings join cleanly to scoped equities; cluster-detection emitters wired into `meta.signal_events` (B-064 follow-up). **Phase 2 progress**: with B-036 closed, **Phase 2 is structurally complete** — the only remaining items in the priority tier are paid-data gated (B-084) or TOS-blocked (B-104). Next priority work is Phase 6 signal-emitter wiring (B-096 macro-regime + B-097 watchlist-scoring) to enable cross-source signal-stack firing.
- **Evidence:** `docs/sources/defillama.md` (~120 lines: chain TVL + protocol TVL + protocol fees/revenue (`fees_usd`, `revenue_usd`) + stablecoins + prices coverage, 20260604 day-alignment migration, Bitcoin exclusion keywords, 5-gate acceptance checklist). `docs/sources/fred.md` (~116 lines: 20-series watchlist across 5 dimensions, vintage-aware PK, vintage chunking + API-key redaction, 5-gate acceptance). `docs/sources/coingecko.md` (~120 lines: three-tier auth, keyless public live-fetch mode, Pro-tier backfill path, 5-gate acceptance). `docs/sources/coinbase.md` (~115 lines: 7 watchlist coins + per-product earliest-availability table, 300-candle cap, column-order quirk, 6-gate acceptance). `docs/sources/cftc.md` (~115 lines: 5-market coverage across TFF + Disaggregated, Tuesday-snapshot/Friday-publication caveat, 5-gate acceptance). `docs/sources/gdelt.md` (~111 lines: 365-day retention policy, watchlist-driven match filter, single-stage pattern, 5-gate acceptance). `docs/sources/yahoo.md` (~112 lines: 3 watchlist groups (equities + benchmarks + ETFs), `adj_close` vs `close` semantics, browser-UA caveat, 6-gate acceptance). `docs/sources/sec.md` (~119 lines: companyfacts + submissions coverage, 200-OK-with-error handling, fair-access UA requirement, 6-gate acceptance). `docs/sources/sec-form4.md` (~123 lines: per-CIK two-phase fetch, 7-letter transaction-code set, soft-failure-per-filing model, 5-gate acceptance). `docs/sources/sec-form13f.md` (~132 lines: 10-filer watchlist, holdings-bearing vs notice-only forms, ×1000 USD conversion, 5-gate acceptance). `docs/sources/onchain-staking.md` (~129 lines: Chainlink v0.2 community + operator pools, cap-and-intent insight, B-116 v0.1 follow-up, 5-gate acceptance). `docs/sources/sui-staking.md` (~119 lines: 129-validator daily snapshot, forward-only constraint, MIST unit choice, 5-gate acceptance). `docs/backlog.md` (B-036 retired). Total: 12 new docs, ~1,531 net lines added.

### ~~B-117 — Sync prompts/skills with shipped CLI surfaces~~
- **Resolved:** 2026-06-12 (branch sync-prompts, commits `9aaa2b2` + `a42b59a` + `9da4004`)
- **Outcome:** First item off Epic E-001 (2026-06-12 codebase-review findings). Fixes the prompt-drift class where the agent-facing methodology prompts — which ARE the researcher's program — lagged behind shipped CLI surfaces in ways that produced wrong behavior, not just friction. **The load-bearing fix:** `prompts/reflect-on-decisions.md` and `.claude/skills/reflect-decisions/SKILL.md` still instructed marking every equity decision `status: deferred` because "equity prices aren't ingested" — stale since B-092 landed `yahoo.candles` and routed equity tickers through `genkei prices`. Because `deferred` is a terminal status the walker skips, the first real reflection run would have silently dropped all equity-core decisions (CRM, VEEV, SaaS-sector) from outcome pairing. Equity decisions now reflect against the adjusted close (`price_usd` carries `adj_close`) with SPY as benchmark (benchmarks route to Yahoo per B-102); deferral is reserved for genuinely unavailable data, stated sleeve-agnostically. **Methodology sync:** `prompts/research-methodology.md` gains `genkei macro-regime` (B-059) as the opening macro read (sessions had been re-deriving the regime by hand from raw series), `genkei stablecoin-flow` (B-108) replacing the "no typed surface yet" raw-SQL guidance, `genkei tvl --protocol <slug>` replacing the "protocol_tvl currently EMPTY" claim (populated for watchlist `protocols:` slugs since B-081), `defillama.protocol_fees` + `genkei revenue-divergence` (B-083/B-062) for the fees/revenue angle, `genkei tvl-drawdown` (B-058, chain-level) for the drawdown edge type, and an equity price-history line in the fundamentals section. `.claude/skills/research/SKILL.md`'s CLI-surface list updated to match. **Process guard:** new "Keeping prompts in sync with the CLI" section in `docs/research/README.md` — any PR that ships/retires a `genkei` subcommand or flips a table EMPTY → populated must grep `prompts/` + `.claude/skills/` for claims it invalidates; phrases like "not yet ingested" / "no typed surface yet" must carry the backlog ID that will obsolete them. Cross-linked from the methodology footer. Every CLI claim in the new text was verified against `src/genkei/cli/` flag definitions during the edit (two drafting errors caught that way: revenue-divergence compares revenue vs token price not fees; tvl-drawdown is chain-level not protocol-level). Tests 1818 passed / 36 skipped, no code changes — docs only. **Unblocks B-118** (reflection-cycle dry run), which would otherwise have exercised the broken defer path.
- **Evidence:** `prompts/reflect-on-decisions.md` (equity outcome pulls via `genkei prices` + SPY benchmark + sleeve-agnostic deferral rule; workhorse-query note covers both asset classes). `.claude/skills/reflect-decisions/SKILL.md` (pre-flight checks `yahoo.candles` health; outcome-pairing equity bullet rewritten; terminal-status wording generalized). `prompts/research-methodology.md` (macro-regime classifier-first guidance in section 1, per-protocol TVL + fees/revenue bullets in section 2, stablecoin-flow + tvl-drawdown in section 3, shortcuts footer + sync reminder). `.claude/skills/research/SKILL.md` (CLI-surface list: macro-regime, tvl --protocol, prices-includes-equities, stablecoin-flow, tvl-drawdown, revenue-divergence). `docs/research/README.md` (prompt/CLI sync checklist with grep recipe + file list).
